Native np, Linux 6.18.40 INET=n, KVM Q35, 2 vCPU, 1 GiB; three alternating runs.
Baseline: core-backed names before SocketWorker refactor; final: worker refactor and core implicit UDP bind.
Same kernel, clients, guest init and service tests. 64 MiB each direction, setup/allocation/integrity included.
No physical Wi-Fi assigned. Rust 1.97.0 release; final incremental disabled after unstable-fingerprint compiler ICE.
